See ",["$","$L11",null,{"href":"#stage-15-runtime-guarantees","children":"Stage 1.5+ runtime guarantees"}]," for the full gap list and #3803 for the convergence roadmap."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","h2",null,{"id":"what-it-gives-you","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-semibold x:target:animate-[fade-in_1.5s] x:mt-10 x:border-b x:pb-1 x:text-3xl nextra-border","children":["What it gives you",["$","$L12",null,{"id":"what-it-gives-you"}]]}],"\n",["$","ul",null,{"className":"x:[:is(ol,ul)_&]:my-[.75em] x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:list-disc x:ms-[1.5em]","children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Built-in Web Shell UI"}]," — ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"qwen serve"}]," serves the browser-based Web Shell at its root (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"http://127.0.0.1:4170/"}],") out of the box; run ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"qwen serve --open"}]," to launch it in your browser automatically. It is served on the same origin as the API, so no second port or reverse proxy is needed. Pass ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"--no-web"}]," for an API-only daemon."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Up to one primary ACP child plus one on-demand child per trusted secondary, many clients"}]," — production attempts to preheat the primary bridge and retries on first use after failure; trusted secondary runtimes start their own child on demand, while untrusted secondaries never start one. Under the default ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"sessionScope: 'single'"}],", clients targeting the same workspace share one ACP session and collaborate on the same conversation, file diffs, and permission prompts."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Reconnect-safe streaming"}]," — SSE with ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"Last-Event-ID"}]," reconnect lets a client drop and pick up exactly where it left off (within the ring’s replay window)."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Paged persisted transcripts"}]," — ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"GET /session/:id/transcript"}]," returns the complete active on-disk transcript as replay pages without attaching a client or changing the live SSE replay window."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"First-responder permissions"}]," — when the agent asks for permission to run a tool, every connected client sees the request; whichever client answers first wins."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"One daemon, one or more workspaces"}]," — repeat ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"--workspace"}]," to register isolated workspace runtimes under one listener. The first workspace is primary and remains the default for requests that omit ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"cwd"}],"."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Experimental daemon-managed channels"}]," — start with ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"qwen serve --channel <name>"}],", or start without a channel and select one later with ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"qwen channel set"}],". Workers are separate processes owned by the daemon lifecycle. Their selection can be queried, replaced, reloaded, and stopped without restarting the daemon."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Remote runtime control"}]," — change a session’s approval mode (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"POST /session/:id/approval-mode"}],"), toggle a tool (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"POST /workspace/tools/:name/enable"}],") or loaded skill (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"POST /workspace/skills/:name/enable"}],") per workspace, scaffold an empty ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"QWEN.md"}]," (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"POST /workspace/init"}],", mechanical only — does NOT call the model; for AI-fill, follow up with ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"POST /session/:id/prompt"}],"), restart a single MCP server with a budget pre-check (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"POST /workspace/mcp/:server/restart"}],"), or add/remove MCP servers at runtime without a daemon restart (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"POST /workspace/mcp/servers"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"DELETE /workspace/mcp/servers/:name"}],"). All strict-gated — configure ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"--token"}]," first."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Session recap"}]," (",["$","$L11",null,{"href":"https://github.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/issues/4175","children":"#4175"}]," follow-up) — fetch a one-sentence “where did I leave off” summary of an active session (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"POST /session/:id/recap"}],"). Wraps core’s ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"generateSessionRecap"}]," as a side-query against the fast model; pollutes neither the main chat history nor the SSE stream. Non-strict gate (same posture as ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"/prompt"}],"); SDK helper ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"client.recapSession(sessionId)"}],".\n",["$","ul",null,{"className":"x:[:is(ol,ul)_&]:my-[.75em] x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:list-disc x:ms-[1.5em]","children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Known limit — token-cost amplification:"}]," the route is a pure-cost endpoint (each call is an LLM side-query, no state benefit) and the daemon has no per-route rate limit in v1. On a no-token loopback default a buggy or malicious local client can spam it to burn tokens. Configure ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"--token"}]," (and optionally ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"--require-auth"}],") on shared dev hosts before exposing the daemon."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Concurrent recap safety:"}]," two simultaneous ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"/recap"}]," calls on the same session run two independent side-queries. ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"generateSessionRecap"}]," reads a snapshot of the chat history via ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"GeminiClient.getChat().getHistory()"}]," and feeds it to a separate ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"BaseLlmClient.generateText"}]," call (via ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"runSideQuery"}],"); it never appends to or mutates the session’s ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"GeminiChat"}],". Safe to call from multiple clients without coordination."]}],"\n"]}],"\n"]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","h2",null,{"id":"v016-alpha-known-limits","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-semibold x:target:animate-[fade-in_1.5s] x:mt-10 x:border-b x:pb-1 x:text-3xl nextra-border","children":["v0.16-alpha known limits",["$","$L12",null,{"id":"v016-alpha-known-limits"}]]}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":["The first npm release of ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"qwen serve"}]," (v0.16-alpha) is intentionally narrow — text-only chat / coding for developers running the daemon on their own machine. ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"workspaceCwd"}]," field surfaces the primary compatibility workspace so clients can intentionally omit It also does not make an allowed tool or shell command\ndeterministic or sandbox its internals; managed deployments must combine the\nprovider decision with their normal tool policy and isolation boundary."}],"\n",["$","blockquote",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:border-gray-300 x:italic x:text-gray-700 x:dark:border-gray-700 x:dark:text-gray-400 x:border-s-2 x:ps-[1.5em]","children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Sizing the load knobs."}]," ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"--max-sessions"}]," is the per-workspace fresh-session cap. ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"--max-total-sessions"}],", when set, is the daemon-wide fresh-session cap.\nThree other layers also limit load — when sizing for a high-concurrency\ndeployment, tune them together:"]}],"\n",["$","ul",null,{"className":"x:[:is(ol,ul)_&]:my-[.75em] x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:list-disc x:ms-[1.5em]","children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"listener-level"}],": ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"--max-connections"}]," / ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"server.maxConnections=256"}],"\nbounds raw TCP connections (slow-client back-pressure)."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"per-session subscribers"}],": the EventBus caps SSE subscribers at\n64 per session by default; the 65th client gets a terminal\n",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"stream_error"}]," and is closed."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"per-session prompt admissions"}],":\n",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"--max-pending-prompts-per-session=5"}]," bounds queued + active prompts\naccepted for one session. The effective session ceiling\nis the lower of any finite daemon-wide cap and the aggregate per-runtime cap\n(treat that aggregate as unlimited if the per-workspace cap is unlimited). If\nneither is finite, there is no finite session ceiling. A finite ceiling × 64\nsubscribers × 256 frames is the worst-case in-flight memory at the EventBus\nlayer; multiplying it by\n",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"--max-pending-prompts-per-session"}]," bounds accepted prompt work at the\nadmission layer. ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"caps.features.allow_origin"}]," is advertised conditionally so SDK / webui clients can pre-flight whether the daemon honors cross-origin hits before issuing them. ",["$","strong",null,{"children":["Browser webuis without ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"--allow-origin"}]," configured"]}]," still fall back to the same Stage 1 options as before: package as a native shell (Electron/Tauri) so no ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"Origin"}]," header is sent, or front the daemon with a same-origin reverse proxy."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Chrome extension browser automation is separate from framing."}]," ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"qwen serve --allow-origin chrome-extension://<id>"}]," lets the extension frame the Web Shell and connect to the daemon. ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"QWEN_SERVER_TOKEN"}]," is scrubbed before spawn because the agent does not need the daemon bearer. ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"This is intentional, not a sandbox."}]," The agent runs as the same UID with shell-tool access, so anything in ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"~/.bashrc"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"~/.aws/credentials"}],", or ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"~/.npmrc"}]," is reachable by prompt injection regardless. Environment isolation between runtimes is not an operating-system security boundary; do not run ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"qwen serve"}]," under an identity that has credentials you would not trust the agent with."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Per-subscriber bounded SSE queues"}]," — a slow client that overflows its queue gets a ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"client_evicted"}]," terminal frame and is closed; one stuck consumer can’t pin the daemon."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Per-session prompt admission cap"}]," — defaults to 5 accepted-but-unsettled prompts per session. A buggy client cannot enqueue unbounded prompt promises or temporary SSE waits for one session."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Graceful shutdown"}]," — SIGINT/SIGTERM drain the agent children before closing the listener (10s deadline per child)."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","blockquote",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:border-gray-300 x:italic x:text-gray-700 x:dark:border-gray-700 x:dark:text-gray-400 x:border-s-2 x:ps-[1.5em]","children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":["⚠️ ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"Stage 1 known gap — permissions are daemon-global, not per-session (BUy4H)."}]," ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"pendingPermissions"}]," lives at daemon scope; any client holding the bearer token can vote on any ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"requestId"}]," for any session it can see (and SSE ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"permission_request"}]," events carry the requestId in their payload). This is acceptable under the single-user / small-team trust model where every authenticated client is the same human or collaborators they trust. Stage 1.5 will move to ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"POST /session/:id/permission/:requestId"}]," + session-scoped pending map + per-client identity (must-have #3 from the downstream review); until then, don’t run ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"qwen serve"}]," behind a bearer shared with untrusted parties."]}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":["⚠️ ",["$","strong",null,{"children":["Stage 1 known gap — ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"POST /session/:id/prompt"}]," body capped at 10 MB (BUy4L)."]}]," Multimodal prompts containing images / PDFs / audio that exceed 10 MB will fail at body-parse time before route logic runs (no streaming, no mid-upload abort). Workaround: shrink the content client-side, or pass a path reference and let the agent read the file via ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"readTextFile"}],". Stage 1.5 will accept ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"multipart/form-data"}]," or chunked encoding on ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"/prompt"}]," so large prompts don’t hit a cliff."]}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":["⚠️ ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"Stage 1 known gap — phantom SSE connections behind NAT."}]," The\ndaemon detects dead clients via TCP back-pressure on heartbeats\n(15s interval). A client that vanishes WITHOUT a TCP RST (e.g. a\nNAT box silently dropping idle flows) keeps the kernel-level socket\n“alive” until Node’s keepalive probes time out — typically ~2 hours\non Linux defaults.
